<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p><strong>All-time views:&nbsp;</strong>318M</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p><strong>Collections:&nbsp;</strong><a href="https://archive.org/details/wikipediaoutlinks">Wikipedia Outlinks</a>,&nbsp;<a href="https://archive.org/details/webwidecrawl">Internet Archive Web Crawls</a>,&nbsp;<a href="https://archive.org/details/web">Web Crawls</a>,&nbsp;<a href="https://archive.org/details/wikicollections">Wiki Collections</a></p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>Crawl of outlinks from wikipedia.org started May, 2011. These files are currently not publicly accessible.</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>จําเป็นต้องมีบัญชีผู้ใช้เพื่อแก้ไขวิกินี้ แต่เราต้องปิดใช้งานการลงทะเบียนผู้ใช้สาธารณะเนื่องจากสแปม</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>หากต้องการขอบัญชี โปรดสอบถามผู้ใช้ที่ยืนยันอัตโนมัติที่&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="https://whatwg.org/chat" target="_blank">แชท</a>&nbsp;(เช่นหนึ่งใน&nbsp;<a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/index.php?title=Special:ListUsers&amp;group=autoconfirmed">สมาชิกที่ยืนยันอัตโนมัติถาวรเหล่านี้</a>)</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:heading {"level":1} -->
<h1 class="wp-block-heading">MetaExtensions</h1>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p><a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/wiki/MetaExtensions#mw-head">กระโดดไปที่การนําทาง</a><a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/wiki/MetaExtensions#searchInput">กระโดดไปค้นหา</a></p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>หน้านี้แสดงรายการค่าส่วนขยายสําหรับแอตทริบิวต์ name="" ขององค์ประกอบ HTML &lt;meta&gt; คุณสามารถเพิ่มค่าของคุณเองลงในรายการนี้ได้</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>หากต้องการความช่วยเหลือในการจัดทําข้อกําหนด โปรดดูที่&nbsp;<a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/wiki/Category:Specification_editing">การแก้ไขข้อกําหนด</a>&nbsp;หมวดหมู่และ&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="https://github.com/tabatkins/bikeshed/" target="_blank">โรงจักรยาน</a>&nbsp;เอกสารประกอบ</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>หากสเปคพร้อมเราขอให้คุณ:</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>หลีกเลี่ยงความซ้ําซ้อน</strong> หากมีคนกําหนดชื่อที่ใกล้เคียงกับที่คุณต้องการแล้ว โปรดนําชื่อนั้นกลับมาใช้ใหม่</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>อย่าลืมรวม <em>ทั้งหมด</em> รายการ</strong> <a href="http://www.whatwg.org/specs/web-apps/current-work/multipage/semantics.html#other-metadata-names" target="_blank" rel="noopener">ระบุไว้ในข้อมูลจําเพาะ</a> <em>รวมถึงลิงก์ไปยังข้อกําหนด</em> ที่ระบุคีย์เวิร์ด <em>เป็นคําหลักเมตา HTML</em>● หากข้อเสนอขาดข้อกําหนดและมีเวอร์ชันในข้อกําหนดเฉพาะที่สมบูรณ์ ข้อเสนอหลังจะเป็นที่ต้องการ</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>เรียงตามตัวอักษรตามคําหลัก</strong>, โดยไม่คํานึงถึงกรณี, ในตารางใด ๆ เรียงลําดับที่ไม่ใช่ตัวอักษรและตัวเลขตามค่า ASCII</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>ไม่จองเนมสเปซ</strong> ให้ลงทะเบียนชื่อแต่ละชื่อแทน (เรียกว่าค่าที่แจกแจง แม้ว่าคุณจะไม่ต้องนับจํานวนก็ตาม)</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>ต้องไม่เพิ่มแอตทริบิวต์ที่มีค่า URL ลงในหน้านี้เป็นชื่อเมตา แต่ควรลงทะเบียนเป็น&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#HTML5_link_type_extensions" target="_blank">คําหลัก rel</a>&nbsp;แทน</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:heading -->
<h2 class="wp-block-heading">เนื้อหา</h2>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/wiki/MetaExtensions#Meta_Name_Extensions">1ส่วนขยายชื่อเมตา</a></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/wiki/MetaExtensions#Proposals_That_Don't_Meet_the_Requirements_For_a_Registration">2ข้อเสนอที่ไม่ตรงตามข้อกําหนดสําหรับการลงทะเบียน</a></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/wiki/MetaExtensions#Failed_Proposals">3ข้อเสนอที่ล้มเหลว</a></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list -->

<!-- wp:heading -->
<h2 class="wp-block-heading">ส่วนขยายชื่อเมตา</h2>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:table -->
<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">คําหลัก</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">คําอธิบายสั้น ๆ</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">เชื่อมโยงไปยังข้อกําหนด</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">คําพ้องความหมาย</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">สถานะ</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.การเข้าถึง</td><td>ข้อความที่ระบุลักษณะการเข้าถึงของทรัพยากร</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.agls.gov.au/pdf/AGLS%20Metadata%20Standard%20Part%202%20Usage%20Guide.PDF" target="_blank">คู่มือการใช้งานข้อมูลเมตาของ AGLS</a></td><td></td><td>ข้อเสนอ</td></tr><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.accessMode</td><td>โหมดการรับรู้สําหรับทรัพยากร</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.agls.gov.au/pdf/AGLS%20Metadata%20Standard%20Part%202%20Usage%20Guide.PDF" target="_blank">คู่มือการใช้งานข้อมูลเมตาของ AGLS</a></td><td></td><td>ข้อเสนอ</td></tr><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.act</td><td>กฎหมายเฉพาะที่กําหนดหรือขับเคลื่อนการสร้างหรือการจัดหาทรัพยากร</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.agls.gov.au/pdf/AGLS%20Metadata%20Standard%20Part%202%20Usage%20Guide.PDF" target="_blank">คู่มือการใช้งานข้อมูลเมตาของ AGLS</a></td><td></td><td>ข้อเสนอ</td></tr><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.aggregationLevel</td><td>ระดับการรวมตัวของทรัพยากรที่อธิบายไว้ - 'รายการ' หรือ 'การรวบรวม'</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.agls.gov.au/pdf/AGLS%20Metadata%20Standard%20Part%202%20Usage%20Guide.PDF" target="_blank">คู่มือการใช้งานข้อมูลเมตาของ AGLS</a></td><td></td><td>ข้อเสนอ</td></tr><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.allow-search</td><td>กฎหมายเฉพาะที่กําหนดหรือขับเคลื่อนการสร้างหรือการจัดหาทรัพยากร</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.agls.gov.au/pdf/AGLS%20Metadata%20Standard%20Part%202%20Usage%20Guide.PDF" target="_blank">คู่มือการใช้งานข้อมูลเมตาของ AGLS</a></td><td></td><td>ข้อเสนอ</td></tr><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.ความพร้อมใช้งาน</td><td>วิธีการรับหรือเข้าถึงทรัพยากรหรือข้อมูลติดต่อ ส่วนใหญ่ใช้สําหรับทรัพยากรออฟไลน์เพื่อให้ข้อมูลเกี่ยวกับวิธีการเข้าถึงทรัพยากรทางกายภาพ</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.agls.gov.au/pdf/AGLS%20Metadata%20Standard%20Part%202%20Usage%20Guide.PDF" target="_blank">คู่มือการใช้งานข้อมูลเมตาของ AGLS</a></td><td></td><td>ข้อเสนอ</td></tr><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.case</td><td>กฎหมายกรณีเฉพาะที่กําหนดหรือขับเคลื่อนการสร้างหรือการจัดหาทรัพยากร</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.agls.gov.au/pdf/AGLS%20Metadata%20Standard%20Part%202%20Usage%20Guide.PDF" target="_blank">คู่มือการใช้งานข้อมูลเมตาของ AGLS</a></td><td></td><td>ข้อเสนอ</td></tr><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.หมวดหมู่</td><td>ประเภททั่วไปของทรัพยากรที่กําลังอธิบาย - 'บริการ', 'เอกสาร' หรือ 'หน่วยงาน'</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.agls.gov.au/pdf/AGLS%20Metadata%20Standard%20Part%202%20Usage%20Guide.PDF" target="_blank">คู่มือการใช้งานข้อมูลเมตาของ AGLS</a></td><td></td><td>ข้อเสนอ</td></tr><tr><td>AGLSTERMS.dateLicensed</td><td>วันที่ใบอนุญาตถูกนําไปใช้หรือมีผลใช้บังคับ</td><td><a rel="noopener" Any named color, or hex color value is valid.&lt;meta name="msapplication-navbutton-color" content="#FF3300" /&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/gg491732(v=VS.85).aspx" target="_blank">Declaring Pinned Site Metadata</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-notification</td><td><strong>"Pinned Sites" and "Live Tiles" in Windows 8.1</strong>Used to make pinned sites on the start screen into live tiles. <strong>Note</strong>&nbsp;The&nbsp;<strong>msapplication-square150x150logo</strong>&nbsp;value is supported as of IE11 Preview and applies to tiles pinned to the Windows Start screen.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/ie/dn255024%28v=vs.85%29.aspx" target="_blank">Pinned site metadata reference</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-square310x310logo</td><td><strong>msapplication-square310x310logo</strong>Specifies the image to use as the large tile, which is 310x310 pixels at 100% scaling.&lt;meta name="msapplication-square310x310logo" content="images/largelogo.png"&gt; <strong>Note</strong>&nbsp;The&nbsp;<strong>msapplication-square310x310logo</strong>&nbsp;value is supported as of IE11 Preview and applies to tiles pinned to the Windows Start screen.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/ie/dn255024%28v=vs.85%29.aspx" target="_blank">Pinned site metadata reference</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-square70x70logo</td><td><strong>msapplication-square70x70logo</strong>Specifies the image to use as the small tile, which is 70x70 pixels at 100% scaling.&lt;meta name="msapplication-square70x70logo" content="images/tinylogo.png"&gt; <strong>Note</strong>&nbsp;The&nbsp;<strong>msapplication-square70x70logo</strong>&nbsp;value is supported as of IE11 Preview and applies to tiles pinned to the Windows Start screen.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/ie/dn255024%28v=vs.85%29.aspx" target="_blank">Pinned site metadata reference</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-starturl</td><td><strong>"Jump List" or "Pinned Sites" in Windows 7</strong>The "msapplication-starturl" metadata contains the root URL of the application. The start URL can be fully qualified, or relative to the current document. Only HTTP and HTTPS protocols are allowed. If this element is missing, the address of the current page is used instead.&lt;meta name="msapplication-starturl" content="./" /&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/gg491732(v=VS.85).aspx" target="_blank">Declaring Pinned Site Metadata</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-tap-highlight</td><td><strong>Link highlighting in Internet Explorer</strong>The "msapplication-tap-highlight" meta tag can be used to disable automatic highlighting of tapped links in Internet Explorer. Applies to IE10 on Windows Phone 8 and IE11 on Windows 8.1.&lt;meta name="msapplication-tap-highlight" content="no" /&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/ie/bg182645%28v=vs.85%29.aspx#tapHighlight" target="_blank">Link highlighting</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-task</td><td><strong>"Jump List" or "Pinned Sites" in Windows 7</strong>Jump List items act as entry points into the website even when the browser is not running. A Jump List can contain commonly used destinations and tasks. Some items apply to the whole site, and some apply only to specific users. For example, to add a single task called "Check Order Status" specify a meta element in the head of your webpage, as follows:&lt;META name="msapplication-task" content="name=Check Order Status; action-uri=./orderStatus.aspx?src=IE9; icon-uri=./favicon.ico" /&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/gg491725(v=vs.85).aspx" target="_blank">Tasks in Jump List</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-TileColor</td><td>The "msapplication-TileColor" metadata define the background color of a tile in Pinned Sites in Windows 8. The tile color can be specified as a hex RGB color using CSS’s #rrggbb notation, via CSS color names, or by the CSS rgb() function.&lt;meta name="msapplication-TileColor" content="#d83434"/&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://blogs.msdn.com/b/ie/archive/2012/06/08/high-quality-visuals-for-pinned-sites-in-windows-8.aspx" target="_blank">High Quality Visuals for Pinned Sites in Windows 8</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-TileImage</td><td>The "msapplication-TileImage" metadata define the path to an image to be used as background for a tile in Pinned Sites in Windows 8. Tile images must be square PNGs 144px by 144px.&lt;meta name="msapplication-TileImage" content="images/benthepcguy-144.png" /&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://blogs.msdn.com/b/ie/archive/2012/06/08/high-quality-visuals-for-pinned-sites-in-windows-8.aspx" target="_blank">High Quality Visuals for Pinned Sites in Windows 8</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-tooltip</td><td><strong>"Jump List" or "Pinned Sites" in Windows 7</strong>The "msapplication-tooltip" metadata provides additional tooltip text that appears when you hover over the Pinned Site shortcut in the Windows Start menu or on the desktop.&lt;meta name="msapplication-tooltip" content="Channel 9 Podcasts" /&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/gg491732(v=VS.85).aspx" target="_blank">Declaring Pinned Site Metadata</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-wide310x150logo</td><td><strong>msapplication-wide310x150logo</strong>Specifies the image to use as the medium tile, which is 150x150 pixels at 100% scaling.&lt;meta name="msapplication-wide310x150logo" content="images/widelogo.png"&gt; <strong>Note</strong>&nbsp;The&nbsp;<strong>msapplication-wide310x150logo</strong>&nbsp;value is supported as of IE11 Preview and applies to tiles pinned to the Windows Start screen.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/ie/dn255024%28v=vs.85%29.aspx" target="_blank">Pinned site metadata reference</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msapplication-window</td><td><strong>"Jump List" or "Pinned Sites" in Windows 7</strong>The "msapplication-window" metadata sets the initial size of the Pinned Site window when it is launched for the first time. However, if the user adjusts the size of the window, the Pinned Site retains the new dimensions when it is launched again. The following properties can be used in the value of the&nbsp;<code>content</code>&nbsp;attribute:width - The window width in pixels. The minimum value is 800.height - The window height in pixels. The minimum value is 600.&lt;meta name="msapplication-window" content="width=1024;height=768" /&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/gg491732(v=VS.85).aspx" target="_blank">Declaring Pinned Site Metadata</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>MSSmartTagsPreventParsing</td><td>Prevent a Microsoft browser from adding a certain kind of content to a page because users could be confused into thinking the added content was part of the original page. (It's likely only relatively few copies of the browser, a beta release never finalized, are still in the installed Win base, but the base is so large that even relatively few would still be a significant absolute quantity.)<br><br>This tag is used by Google:&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="https://support.google.com/customsearch/answer/2595557?hl=en" target="_blank">Custom Search Help: Meta Tags</a>, as accessed 1-8-16. And "&lt;meta content='true' name='MSSmartTagsPreventParsing'/&gt;" appeared (with internal quote marks as singles) in the source code for &lt;<a rel="noopener" href="http://googleblog.blogspot.com/2009/04/listening-to-google-health-users.html" target="_blank">[9]</a>&gt;, as accessed 4-27-09.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://brittlebit.org/specifications/html-meta-mssmarttagspreventparsing/specification-for-html-meta-element-with-name-value-mssmarttagspreventparsing.html" target="_blank">BrittleBit.org spec</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>msvalidate.01</td><td>One of the verification elements used by Bing.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://onlinehelp.microsoft.com/en-us/bing/hh204490.aspx" target="_blank">Bing Webmaster Tools</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>nonfiction</td><td>To reduce the "fake news" problem, this lets well-meaning page authors label satire and parody appropriately in metatags although unlabeled in content (not explicitly labeling being common in satire and parody). Characterize the principal content of the page as nonfiction, fiction, mixed, or other. This way, other websites and users needn't be fooled.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://brittlebit.org/specifications/html-meta-nonfiction/specification-for-html-meta-element-with-name-value-nonfiction.html" target="_blank">BrittleBit.org spec</a></td><td>fiction (antonym; see spec for usage)</td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>norton-safeweb-site-verification</td><td>Used to verify ownership of Website for Norton SafeWeb.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://safeweb.norton.com/help/site_owners#verification_tips" target="_blank">Norton SafeWeb Help Documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>origin</td><td>The&nbsp;<em>origin</em>&nbsp;meta tag is used to indicate the original source of a document.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://doc.ohreally.nl/metatag-origin" target="_blank">The&nbsp;<em>origin</em>&nbsp;meta tag</a></td><td></td><td>Proposed</td></tr><tr><td>origin-trials</td><td>Used to enable experimental browser features for sites which are registered to participate in experimental trials.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://docs.google.com/document/d/1v5fi0EUV_QHckVHVF2K4P72iNywnrJtNhNZ6i2NPt0M/edit?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Experimental Framework Trial Token Structure</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>p:domain_verify</td><td>Used to register a site's domain with Pinterest as a "verified domain".Example: <code>&lt;meta name="p:domain_verify" content="5dd1c5f2db0ac0b521f08d56b4cd271b"&gt;</code></td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://help.pinterest.com/entries/22488487-Verify-with-HTML-meta-tags" target="_blank">Pinterest Help Article</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>page-version</td><td>When a page uploaded two or more times with the same last-modification date (per dc.modified, dcterms.modified, or article:modified_time metatag), distinguishing different uploads even on the same date can be done with the page-version metatag.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://brittlebit.org/specifications/html-meta-page-version/specification-for-html-meta-element-with-name-value-page-version.html" target="_blank">BrittleBit.org spec</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>pingdom</td><td>Used by Pingdom monitoring services as a heartbeat verification. The heartbeat service will only consider the request successful if it contains the "pingdom" meta tag, with the content set according to a per-customer key.Example:<code>&lt;meta name="pingdom" content="6bh3nxnx"/&gt;</code></td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>pinterest</td><td>Used to block pinterest from linking to content on the URL.Example:<code>&lt;meta name="pinterest" content="nopin"/&gt;</code></td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://support.pinterest.com/entries/21101932-what-if-i-don-t-want-images-from-my-site-to-be-pinned" target="_blank">Pinterest Help Article</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>prism:alternateTitle</td><td>An alternate title or alternate headline for a resource that may be used in a table of contents, a popup etc. and can vary with platform.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.prismstandard.org/specifications/3.0/PRISM_Basic_Metadata_3.0.htm" target="_blank">PRISM Basic Metadata Specification V3.0</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>pro</td><td>Signify expertise as judged by an authority. Counterpart to pro-auth (which see). Useful to search engines and Web directories.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://brittlebit.org/specifications/html-meta-pro/specification-for-html-meta-element-with-name-value-pro.html" target="_blank">BrittleBit.org spec</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>pro-auth</td><td>Signify an authority, subject to search engine's or directory's judgment of importance and credibility (the judgment could be by a human working for a search engine), so the authority's listing of experts can carry weight, making search results more useful.<br><br>Counterpart to pro (which see). Supported also by pro-auth-field (which see) and pro-auth-fragment (which see).</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://brittlebit.org/specifications/html-meta-pro-auth/specification-for-html-meta-element-with-name-value-pro-auth.html" target="_blank">BrittleBit.org spec</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>pro-auth-field</td><td>Signify an authority's claimed field of expertise, importance and validity to be judged by search engine or directory. Supports pro-auth (which see).</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://brittlebit.org/specifications/html-meta-pro-auth-field/specification-for-html-meta-element-with-name-value-pro-auth-field.html" target="_blank">BrittleBit.org spec</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>pro-auth-fragment</td><td>Signify which links from authority are links to experts, as distinct from other links. Supports pro-auth (which see).</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://brittlebit.org/specifications/html-meta-pro-auth-fragment/specification-for-html-meta-element-with-name-value-pro-auth-fragment.html" target="_blank">BrittleBit.org spec</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>rating</td><td>The Restricted to Adults label (RTA) provides a way for adult oriented websites to indicate that their content is off limits to children. RTA was introduced in 2006 and is currently used by a large number of adult web content providers. RTA is recognized by all major parental control filters.Example:&lt;meta name="RATING" content="RTA-5042-1996-1400-1577-RTA" /&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.rtalabel.org/index.php?content=howto" target="_blank">RTA documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>referrer</td><td>Controls whether the user agent includes the Referer header in HTTP requests originating from this document</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://www.w3.org/TR/referrer-policy/" target="_blank">Referrer Policy</a></td><td>referer</td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>RepostUsAPIKey</td><td>Used to verify ownership of Website for Repost syndication service</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.repost.us/meta-headers-used-by-repost/" target="_blank">Meta Headers used by Repost</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles</td><td><a href="http://www.mediawiki.org/wiki/">MediaWiki</a>'s&nbsp;<a href="http://www.mediawiki.org/wiki/ResourceLoader">ResourceLoader</a>&nbsp;uses this name with&nbsp;<code>content</code>&nbsp;set to the empty string. The purpose is to mark the DOM position before which dynamic styles should be added.</td><td><a href="http://www.mediawiki.org/wiki/ResourceLoader/ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles_specification">ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>review_date</td><td>The date a resource is scheduled for review by content creator in order to determine if it should be archived, updated or retained as is.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.hc-sc.gc.ca/home-accueil/alt_formats/pacrb-dgapcr/pdf/Metadata_Application_Profile_2009.pdf" target="_blank">Health Canada Web Metadata Application Profile March 2009</a><a rel="noopener" href="http://lists.w3.org/Archives/Public/www-archive/2014Feb/att-0020/dnd_ims-6001-1-2-eng.pdf" target="_blank">IMS 6001-1-2, Metadata for Recordkeeping and Web Content</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>revision</td><td>The revision of this page as reported by an underlying Version Control System. This is a free format string.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://github.com/krallin/meta-revision" target="_blank">Meta Revision Specification</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>revisit-after</td><td>revisit-after is used to tell search engines how often to recrawl the page. To our knowledge only one search engine has ever supported it, and that search engine was never widely used — at this point, it is nothing more than a good luck charm.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://code.google.com/webstats/2005-12/metadata.html" target="_blank">Google documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>rights</td><td>Assert legal rights for the page in a machine-parsable format. Rights could include those in copyright, for trade secrets, and in other intellectual property.<br><br><a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/wiki/Talk:MetaExtensions#rights:_why_reversion">Talk</a></td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://brittlebit.org/specifications/html-meta-rights/specification-for-html-meta-element-with-name-value-rights.html" target="_blank">BrittleBit.org spec</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>rights-standard</td><td>The purpose is to enable search engines and other cataloging services to compile the types of rights allocated to the work. (Does any search engine actually implement this?&nbsp;<a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/index.php?title=User:Hsivonen&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">hsivonen</a>&nbsp;07:34, 14 July 2011 (UTC))<strong>This keyword does not provide, remove or alter any legal protections or designations.</strong>Format:&lt;meta name="rights-standard" content="element id;rights" /&gt;element id - the HTML Element ID of the item these rights apply torights - what rights are assigned to the item"pd" - Public domain"cc by-sa" - Creative Commons Attribution"cc by-nd" - Creative Commons NoDerivs"cc by-nc" - Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial"cc by-nc-sa" - Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial-ShareAlike"cc by-nc-nd" - Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial-NoDerivs</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://sites.google.com/site/metarightsstandard/" target="_blank">Spec</a></td><td>Redundant with&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://www.whatwg.org/specs/web-apps/current-work/multipage/microdata.html#licensing-works" target="_blank">Microdata vocabulary for licensing works</a>.</td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>robots</td><td>A comma-separated list of operators explaining how search engine crawlers should treat the content. This meta name is already supported by every popular search engine.<br>Possible values are "noarchive" to prevent cached versions, "noindex" to prevent indexing, and "nofollow" works as the link rel value with the same name.<br>The content value "NOODP" has been offered elsewhere, so I'm proposing it here. It blocks robots from using&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://www.dmoz.org/" target="_blank">Open Directory Project</a>&nbsp;descriptions of a website instead of Web pages' own meta descriptions. It may have been introduced by Microsoft.<br>The content value "NOYDIR" has been offered by Yahoo, so I'm proposing it here. It blocks Yahoo's robot from using the Yahoo directory's descriptions of a website instead of Web pages' own meta descriptions. Whether any other robot supports this is unknown but possibly no other search engine uses Yahoo's directory anyway.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.robotstxt.org/meta.html" target="_blank">About the Robots &lt;META&gt; tag</a>; NOODP value:&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://www.google.com/support/webmasters/bin/answer.py?hl=en&amp;answer=35264" target="_blank">Google</a>,&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://help.yahoo.com/l/us/yahoo/search/indexing/indexing-11.html" target="_blank">Yahoo</a>, NOYDIR value:&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://ysearchblog.com/2007/02/28/yahoo-search-support-for-noydir-meta-tags-and-weather-update/" target="_blank">Yahoo</a>, as accessed 4-28-09</td><td>baiduspider (for targeting Baidu or Baiduspider only)<br>citeseerxbot (for targeting CiteSeerX only)<br>googlebot (for targeting Googlebot only)<br>googlebot-mobile (for targeting Googlebot-Mobile)<br>ia_archive (for targeting Internet Archive (Wayback Machine) or Alexa only)<br>msnbot (for targeting Microsoft or Bing only)<br>slurp (for targeting Yahoo or slurp only)<br>teoma (for targeting TEOMA or Ask.com card type, which will be one of "summary", "photo", "app", or "player".</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:creator</td><td>@username for the content creator / author.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:creator:id</td><td>Twitter ID for the content creator / author.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:data1</td><td>String value; value for labels such as price, items in stock, sizes, etc</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:data2</td><td>String value; value for labels such as price, items in stock, sizes, etc</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:description</td><td>A description of the content in a maximum of 200 characters.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:domain</td><td>Short URL assigned by Twitter to a website.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:image</td><td>A URL to the image representing the content.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:image0</td><td>A URL to the image representing the first photo in your gallery.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:image1</td><td>A URL to the image representing the second photo in your Image must be less than 1MB in size.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:image:width</td><td>The width of the image representing the content.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:label1</td><td>String value; label such as price, items in stock, sizes, etc</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:label2</td><td>String value; label such as price, items in stock, sizes, etc</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:player</td><td>HTTPS URL to iframe player. This must be a HTTPS URL which does not generate active mixed content warnings in a web browser</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:player:height</td><td>Height of IFRAME specified in twitter:player in pixels</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:player:stream</td><td>URL to raw stream that will be rendered in Twitter's mobile applications directly</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:player:stream:content_type</td><td>The MIME type/subtype combination that describes the content contained in twitter:player:stream</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:player:width</td><td>Width of IFRAME specified in twitter:player in pixels</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:site</td><td>@username for the website used in the card footer.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:site:id</td><td>Twitter ID for the website used in the card footer.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:title</td><td>The title of the content as it should appear in the card.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>twitter:url</td><td>Canonical URL of the card content.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>typemetal.formatprefs</td><td>Per-file HTML formatting preferences used by the TypeMetal HTML editor</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://coherencelabs.com/typemetal/manual/typemetal-custom-metadata.html" target="_blank">TypeMetal User Guide</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>verify-v1</td><td>Superseded by google-site-verification. Legacy verification for Google Sitemaps.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://sitemaps.blogspot.com/2006/05/more-about-meta-tag-verification.html" target="_blank">Inside Google Sitemaps: More about meta tag verification</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>version</td><td>The version of a web application according to the&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://semver.org/" target="_blank">Semantic Versioning</a>&nbsp;specification<br>Example:<code>&lt;meta name="version" content="0.1.0+1"&gt;</code></td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://github.com/dvorapa/meta-version" target="_blank">Documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>vfb-version</td><td>Specifies a Visual Form Builder plugin version for Wordpress.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://wordpress.org/plugins/visual-form-builder/" target="_blank">Visual Form Builder Documentation and specs</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>viewport</td><td>Provides a way for documents to specify (using markup rather than CSS) the size, zoom factor, and orientation of the viewport that is used as the base for the document's&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://www.w3.org/TR/CSS21/visudet.html#containing-block-details" target="_blank">initial containing block</a>. The following properties can be used in the value of the&nbsp;<code>content</code>&nbsp;attribute:widthheightinitial-scaleminimum-scalemaximum-scaleuser-scalableExamples:&lt;meta name="viewport" content="initial-scale=1.0"&gt; &lt;meta name="viewport" content="width=480, initial-scale=2.0, user-scalable=1"&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://dev.w3.org/csswg/css-device-adapt/#viewport-meta" target="_blank">CSS Device Adaptation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>web_author</td><td>Credits the developer(s) responsible for the technical design of a website.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.metatags.info/meta_name_webauthor" target="_blank">Documentation</a></td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://sites.google.com/site/metadesignerspec/" target="_blank">designer</a>&nbsp;- for visual presentation</td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>witget</td><td>Used to verify ownership for Witget.com.Example &lt;meta name="witget" content="XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX"&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://support.witget.com/topic/435278-prostaya-ustanovka-skripta/" target="_blank">Witget manual</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>wot-verification</td><td>Used to verify ownership of WOT (Web Of Trust)</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.mywot.com/wiki/Verify_your_website" target="_blank">WOT's verify your site wiki page</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>wt.ac</td><td><strong>Advertising Click parameter</strong>When a visitor to your site clicks on an ad, that action is referred to as an Ad Click. The following META tag tracks advertising clicks:&lt;META NAME="WT.ac" CONTENT="name"&gt; Defines the name of the advertisement clicked to reach a particular web page. The Ad Click must contain an external redirect back to the client. The redirect needs to include the necessary code to generate a hit to the SDC server. You can designate multiple Advertising Clicks using semicolons. Examples:&lt;a href="file111.html?WT.ac=CONTENT111"&gt; &lt;a href="file222.html?WT.ac=CONTENT222"&gt; The name of the advertisement clicked to reach a particular web page. To capture this information with DCS, the Advertising Click must contain an external redirect back to the client. The redirect needs toinclude the necessary code to generate a hit to the DCS. The maximum length for each name is 64 bytes.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>wt.ad</td><td><strong>Advertising View parameter</strong>Visitors often view advertisements that they do not necessarily click on. You can use On-Site Advertising to determine the number of visitors to your web site who view particular ads. With this feature you can produce advertising reports for each of your clients. If you are selling advertising space on your web site, for example, you can collect traffic statistics to help determine pricing schedules. The following META tag tracks advertising views:&lt;meta name="WT.ad" content="My content"&gt; An Ad View occurs when a visitor views a page containing an ad. An ad is a link or graphic that contains an Ad Click parameter in the query portion of it's URL.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>wt.cg_n</td><td><strong>Name of the Content Group</strong>Used to configure the appropriate Webtrends advanced feature. These are just some of the more popular ones. These appear in your META tags. – showing you the web page, the source (meta tag), the log files entry and the subsequent WT report. Example:&lt;meta name="wt.cg_n" content="My content"&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>wt.cg_s</td><td><strong>Name of Content Sub-Group</strong>Used to configure the appropriate Webtrends advanced feature. These are just some of the more popular ones. These appear in your META tags. – showing you the web page, the source (meta tag), the log files entry and the subsequent WT report. Example:&lt;meta name="wt.cg_s" content="My content"&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>wt.mc_id</td><td><strong>Identifies the ID of the marketing campaign</strong>To attract new students, a university launches a marketing campaign by sending recruitment email to all graduating high school seniors in a metropolitan area. The email links to a special landing page in the university’s web site, containing the following META tag to track marketing campaigns. Example:&lt;META NAME="WT.mc_id" CONTENT="1X2GG34"&gt; You may use this parameter on the URL. Example:&lt;a href="link?WT.mc_id=1X2GG34"&gt; The Campaign ID 1X2GG34 represents recruits to be contacted by email</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>WT.si_n</td><td><strong>Scenario analysis parameter - scenario name</strong>This defines a scenario name for the page or set of pages to be included in the scenario. This in turn produces a funnel type report in Webtrends. Example:&lt;meta name="WT.si_n" content="my_scenario_name"&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a>.</td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>WT.si_p</td><td><strong>Scenario analysis parameter - scenario step name</strong>This defines a scenario step name for the page or set of pages to be included in the scenario. This in turn produces a funnel type report in Webtrends. It works when paired with metedata tag name WT.si_n. Example:&lt;meta name="WT.si_p" content="my_scenario_step_name"&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a>.</td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>WT.si_x</td><td><strong>Scenario analysis parameter - scenario step number</strong>This defines a scenario step number for the page or set of pages to be included in the scenario. This in turn produces a funnel type report in Webtrends. It works when paired with metedata tag name WT.si_n, and as an alternative to Wt.si_p. Example:&lt;meta name="WT.si_x" content="my_scenario_step_number"&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a>.</td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>wt.sv</td><td><strong>Tracking Servers parameter</strong>If your site is hosted on multiple servers, a server cluster, or a server farm, and you want to evaluate the performance of your load balancer, Webtrends can track page views for each server. To do so, populate the following META tag on all pages on each server: Example:&lt;meta name="WT.sv" content="My Server"&gt; Defines the name of the machine that serves the web page. If you have two servers (Server1 and Server2), you would make two copies of the META tag and designate CONTENT=“Server1” for deployment to pages on the first server and CONTENT=“Server2” for deployment to the same pages on the second server. For a server farm, you can extract the value of the built-in server name and dynamically assign it to the META tag using server-side scripting. Example:&lt;meta name="WT.sv" content="Server1"&gt; &lt;meta name="WT.sv" content="Server2"&gt; An Ad View occurs when a visitor views a page containing an ad. An ad is a link or graphic that contains an Ad Click parameter in the query portion of it's URL.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://tagbuilder.webtrends.com/Help/Miscellaneous/AdSearch.aspx?keepThis=true&amp;TB_iframe=true&amp;height=450&amp;width=650" target="_blank">About WT.ad</a>.</td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>wt.ti</td><td><strong>Tracking Page Titles</strong>You may want to modify a page title before sending it to Webtrends in the following cases:You are dealing with dynamic content pages identified by URL parameters, and the page title represents the title of the base URL page rather than the dynamic content page.Unless you modify the page titles, all pages have the same title in the reports.All pages have been assigned the same title, for reasons of style or company policy.Even though URLs are displayed in addition to page title, the entire URL cannot be depended upon to distinguish one page from another. Use server-side scripts to change the title to something that reflects the content of the pages so that you can identify them in reports. Next, pass the customized page titles to Webtrends, using the following META tag:&lt;META NAME="WT.ti" CONTENT="title"&gt; Defines the name of the title for this page.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://product.webtrends.com/WRC/OnDemand/ResourceCenter/rc/Library/PDF/IGOD/WebTrendsAnalyticsOnDemandImplementationGuide.pdf" target="_blank">Webtrends Parameters</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>y_key</td><td>Used to verify ownership for Yahoo! Site Explorer</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://help.yahoo.com/l/us/yahoo/search/siteexplorer/siteexplorer-06.html" target="_blank">Yahoo! documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>yandex-verification</td><td>Used to verify ownership for Yandex Webmaster.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://help.yandex.ru/webmaster/?id=995300#995356" target="_blank">Yandex Webmaster ownership verification</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>ZOOMCATEGORY</td><td>Category of page to be grouped in Wrensoft Zoom Search Engine.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.wrensoft.com/zoom/zoommeta.html" target="_blank">Wrensoft Zoom Meta Documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>ZOOMDESCRIPTION</td><td>Alternative page description for Wrensoft Zoom Search Engine.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.wrensoft.com/zoom/zoommeta.html" target="_blank">Wrensoft Zoom Meta Documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>ZOOMIMAGE</td><td>URL to image to be displayed alongside result in Wrensoft Zoom Search Engine.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.wrensoft.com/zoom/zoommeta.html" target="_blank">Wrensoft Zoom Meta Documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>ZOOMPAGEBOOST</td><td>Page boost factor to increase or decrease the relevance of page in Wrensoft Zoom Search Engine.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.wrensoft.com/zoom/zoommeta.html" target="_blank">Wrensoft Zoom Meta Documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>ZOOMTITLE</td><td>Alternative page title for Wrensoft Zoom Search Engine.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.wrensoft.com/zoom/zoommeta.html" target="_blank">Wrensoft Zoom Meta Documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr><tr><td>ZOOMWORDS</td><td>Additional keywords to be indexed for Wrensoft Zoom Search Engine.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.wrensoft.com/zoom/zoommeta.html" target="_blank">Wrensoft Zoom Meta Documentation</a></td><td></td><td>Proposal</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>

<!-- wp:heading -->
<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Proposals That Don't Meet the&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://www.whatwg.org/specs/web-apps/current-work/multipage/semantics.html#other-metadata-names" target="_blank">Requirements</a>&nbsp;For a Registration</h2>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>Note that these proposals can be moved back to the registry table if the problems listed in the rightmost column of this table are addressed.</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:table -->
<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Keyword</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Brief description</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Link to specification</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Synonyms</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Status</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Registration requirement failure</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>blogcatalog</td><td>Used to verify ownership of Blog Catalog.com</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.blogcatalog.com/" target="_blank">Blog catalog site</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>Claimed spec link is not a link to a spec</td></tr><tr><td>created</td><td>The datetime at which the document was created. The value is an ISO8601 date. The date MUST follow the&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://www.w3.org/TR/NOTE-datetime" target="_blank">W3C Profile of ISO 8601</a>&nbsp;with a granularity of "Complete date:" or finer. The&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://www.bbc.co.uk/guidelines/futuremedia/desed/previousversions/searchmetadata_vs_1_0.shtml#metadata" target="_blank">BBC</a>&nbsp;use this name.</td><td></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>Lacks link to a spec</td></tr><tr><td>expires</td><td><code>meta name='expires'</code>&nbsp;defines the expiration date of the page. This can be used for web pages in preparation for an upcoming event, e.g. a registration form for an exposition or competition, or other cases with a pre-set date when the document will no longer be valid, e.g. a product offer in a special sale or a support page for a product known not to be supported anymore from a given time onward.Search engines should respond to this meta tag in a reasonable way, i.e. by removing the page from their main search results after the expiration date (possibly still returning the result in a special search for expired pages as long as the page exists and is not explicitly excluded via&nbsp;<code>robots.txt</code>&nbsp;or&nbsp;<code>meta name='robots'</code>&nbsp;etc.) or simply by indicating to the user that this result is out-of-date.The content attribute should define the expiration date in accordance with&nbsp;<a rel="noopener" href="http://www.w3.org/TR/NOTE-datetime" target="_blank">http://www.w3.org/TR/NOTE-datetime</a>&nbsp;. The meta tag should not be used for pages without expiration date. However, for historical reasons, search engines should also interpret other date formats where possible and should be prepared to find values such as "", "0", "no" and "never". Such non-date values are to be interpreted as no expiration date.Correctly formatted example:<code>&lt;meta name='expires' content='2012-12-31T23:59Z'&gt;</code>This tag is not to be confused with and has a different meaning than&nbsp;<code>meta http-equiv='expires'.</code></td><td></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>Lacks link to a spec</td></tr><tr><td>fb:admins</td><td>Open Graph Protocol by Facebook developers</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">Facebook developers</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>The spec specifies this to be a value of the property attribute--not a meta keyword</td></tr><tr><td>fb:page_id</td><td>Open Graph Protocol by Facebook developers</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">Facebook developers</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>The spec specifies this to be a value of the property attribute--not a meta keyword</td></tr><tr><td>gm-gpx-v</td><td>Wordpress Plugin Google Maps GPX Viewer</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://wordpress.org/extend/plugins/google-maps-gpx-viewer/" target="_blank">Google Maps GPX Viewer</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>Claimed spec link is not a link to a spec</td></tr><tr><td>google-translate-customization</td><td>Used to verify ownership for Website Translator. &lt;meta name="google-translate-customization" content="Your Website Code Goes Here. Generated When Adding The Google Translate Plugin To Your Site" /&gt;</td><td></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>Lacks link to a spec</td></tr><tr><td>nextgen</td><td>Used for nextgen gallery plugin in wordpress</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.alexa.com/faqs/?p=188" target="_blank">Alexa FAQ About this meta attribute Reference</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>Unrelated spec link</td></tr><tr><td>nibbler-site-verification</td><td>Used to verify ownership of Nibbler site</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://nibbler.silktide.com/" target="_blank">Nibbler site</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>Claimed spec link does not link to a spec</td></tr><tr><td>og:description</td><td>Open Graph Protocol by Facebook developers</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">Facebook developers</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>The spec specifies this to be a value of the property attribute--not a meta keyword</td></tr><tr><td>og:image</td><td>Open Graph Protocol by Facebook developers</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">Facebook developers</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>The spec specifies this to be a value of the property attribute--not a meta keyword</td></tr><tr><td>og:site_name</td><td>Open Graph Protocol by Facebook developers</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">Facebook developers</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>The spec specifies this to be a value of the property attribute--not a meta keyword</td></tr><tr><td>og:title</td><td>Open Graph Protocol by Facebook developers</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">Facebook developers</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>The spec specifies this to be a value of the property attribute--not a meta keyword</td></tr><tr><td>og:type</td><td>Open Graph Protocol by Facebook developers</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">Facebook developers</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>The spec specifies this to be a value of the property attribute--not a meta keyword</td></tr><tr><td>og:url</td><td>Open Graph Protocol by Facebook developers</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">Facebook developers</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>The spec specifies this to be a value of the property attribute--not a meta keyword</td></tr><tr><td>property="og:*"</td><td>Metadata used by the Open Graph protocol (used by Facebook). Note: currently these are defined as: &lt;meta property="og.*" content="x"/&gt;</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://developers.facebook.com/docs/opengraph/" target="_blank">FAQ About the Open Graph protocol from Facebook.</a></td><td></td><td>Doesn't belong in this registry</td><td>Not a value to be used in the&nbsp;<code>name</code>&nbsp;attribute</td></tr><tr><td>resolutions</td><td>Authoring web sites to use resolution independent images that display beautifully on high-resolution displays should be made as easy as possible for developers and should not require JavaScript to accomplish.To accomplish this, I propose a new HTML Meta Tag,&nbsp;<code>resolutions</code>, that can be used to specify that high-resolution versions of images linked to from the page are available and that the browser should use them in place of the lower-resolution default images if it detects that a user is using a high-resolution screen. The resolutions meta tag lists the device-pixel ratios supported by images in the page.So, for example…<code>&lt;meta name="resolutions" content="2x"&gt;</code>… means that the developer is telling the browser that she has created 2x resolution images for the images linked to from the current page and named them with a @2x suffix.To illustrate, if her image tag is as follows…<code>&lt;img src="/images/flower.jpg" alt="A flower"&gt;</code>… then she has two image files under /images: the low-resolution default (flower.jpg), and a higher-resolution (200%) version named&nbsp;flower@2x.jpg.(This is the same naming convention already used by Apple in its Cocoa Touch framework for automatically loading in higher-resolution versions of images.)Based on the meta tag, if the browser detects that the user is running at a&nbsp;<code>min-device-pixel-ratio</code>&nbsp;of 2.0, it will automatically ask for the 2x version of the image (flower@2x.jpg) instead of the default image as specified in the image tag.Finally, so as not to flood external sites with high-resolution image requests, this functionality would only work for local images specified via relative links.<strong>Multiple resolutions</strong>The resolutions tag can also contain a list of supported device-pixel ratios so as to support even higher-resolution displays when and if they become available in the future.For example:<code>&lt;meta name="resolutions" content="2x, 4x, 8x"&gt;</code>In this case, the developer would provide 2x, 4x, and 8x versions of all images. So, in the running example, she would make flower.jpg,&nbsp;flower@2x.jpg,&nbsp;flower@4x.jpg, and&nbsp;flower@8x.jpg.<strong>Advantages</strong>The advantages of this approach are several:Makes it very simple for developers to support high-resolution displays like the iPhone 4's Retina screenDoes not require JavaScriptDoes not change the default way that things work (if the meta tag is not specified, the browser simply behaves as it always has).</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://aralbalkan.com/3355" target="_blank">Proposal for native browser support of high-resolution image substitution</a><a rel="noopener" href="http://aralbalkan.com/3331" target="_blank">How to make your web content look stunning on the iPhone 4’s new Retina display</a></td><td></td><td>Incomplete proposal</td><td>Lacks link to a spec</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
<!-- /wp:table -->

<!-- wp:heading -->
<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Failed Proposals</h2>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:table -->
<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Keyword</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Brief description</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Link to more details</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Synonyms</th><th class="has-text-align-center" data-align="center">Status</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>bot-.&nbsp;.&nbsp;.</td><td></td><td><a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/index.php?title=MetaExtensions&amp;diff=3727&amp;oldid=3705">Old MetaExtensions revision diff</a>&nbsp;&amp;&nbsp;<a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/index.php?title=MetaExtensions&amp;diff=4088&amp;oldid=3824">edit</a></td><td></td><td>Unendorsed</td></tr><tr><td>cache</td><td>This doesn't actually work; use HTTP headers instead.<br>Value must be "public", "private", or "no-cache". Intended as a simple way to tell user agents whether to store a copy of the document or not. An alternate for HTTP/1.1's cache-control; for publishers without access to modifying cache-control.</td><td>none</td><td></td><td>Unendorsed</td></tr><tr><td>DC.</td><td>Dublin Core, maintained by Dublin Core MetaData Initiative (DCMI), is an extensive system with some overlap with non-DC names.<br>This reserves all strings that begin with DC and a dot.&nbsp;<em>Not true; DC-HTML doesn't use hardwired prefixes, but defines the prefixes using link/@rel="scheme.prefix"</em></td><td><a rel="noopener" href="http://www.dublincore.org/" target="_blank">DCMI</a></td><td></td><td>Unendorsed</td></tr><tr><td>dir-content-pointer</td><td>No longer useful. The function is better performed with HTML markup.</td><td><a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/index.php?title=MetaExtensions&amp;diff=3727&amp;oldid=3705">Old MetaExtensions revision diff</a>&nbsp;&amp;&nbsp;<a href="https://wiki.whatwg.org/index.php?title=MetaExtensions&amp;diff=10213&amp;oldid=10212">edit</a></td><td></td><td>Unendorsed</td></tr><tr><td>no-email-collection</td><td>HTML5 prohibits URL-valued meta names. They should be rel keywords instead.<br>Intended to reference legal policy of web site indicating that harvesting of e-mail addresses on the site is not permitted and in violation of applicable laws such as the CAN-SPAM Act of 2003.</td><td><a rel="noopener" href="https://www.projecthoneypot.org/how_to_avoid_spambots_5.php" target="_blank">Project Honey Pot</a></td><td></td><td>Unendorsed</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>

<!-- wp:template-part {"slug":"footer","theme":"pub/assembler"} /--> 
This repository hosts the [DOM Standard](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/).

## Code of conduct

We are committed to providing a friendly, safe, and welcoming environment for all. Please read and respect the [Code of Conduct](https://whatwg.org/code-of-conduct).

## Contribution opportunities

Folks notice minor and larger issues with the DOM Standard all the time and we'd love your help fixing those. Pull requests for typographical and grammar errors are also most welcome.

Issues labeled ["good first issue"](https://github.com/whatwg/dom/labels/good%20first%20issue) are a good place to get a taste for editing the DOM Standard. Note that we don't assign issues and there's no reason to ask for availability either, just provide a pull request.

If you are thinking of suggesting a new feature, read through the [FAQ](https://whatwg.org/faq) and [Working Mode](https://whatwg.org/working-mode) documents to get yourself familiarized with the process.

We'd be happy to help you with all of this [on Chat](https://whatwg.org/chat).

## Pull requests

In short, change `dom.bs` and submit your patch, with a [good commit message](https://github.com/whatwg/meta/blob/main/COMMITTING.md).

Please add your name to the Acknowledgments section in your first pull request, even for trivial fixes. The names are sorted lexicographically.

To ensure your patch meets all the necessary requirements, please also see the [Contributor Guidelines](https://github.com/whatwg/meta/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md). Editors of the DOM Standard are expected to follow the [Maintainer Guidelines](https://github.com/whatwg/meta/blob/main/MAINTAINERS.md).

## Tests

Tests are an essential part of the standardization process and will need to be created or adjusted as changes to the standard are made. Tests for the DOM Standard can be found in the `dom/` directory of [`web-platform-tests/wpt`](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt).

A dashboard showing the tests running against browser engines can be seen at [wpt.fyi/results/dom](https://wpt.fyi/results/dom).

## Building "locally"

For quick local iteration, run `make`; this will use a web service to build the standard, so that you don't have to install anything. See more in the [Contributor Guidelines](https://github.com/whatwg/meta/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md#building).

## Formatting

Use a column width of 100 characters.

Do not use newlines inside "inline" elements, even if that means exceeding the column width requirement.
```html
<p>The
<dfn method for=DOMTokenList lt=remove(tokens)|remove()><code>remove(<var>tokens</var>&hellip;)</code></dfn>
method, when invoked, must run these steps:
```
is okay and
  ```html
<p>The <dfn method for=DOMTokenList
lt=remove(tokens)|remove()><code>remove(<var>tokens</var>&hellip;)</code></dfn> method, when
invoked, must run these steps:
```
is not.

Using newlines between "inline" element tag names and their content is also forbidden. (This actually alters the content, by adding spaces.) That is
```html
<a>token</a>
```
is fine and
```html
<a>token
</a>
```
is not.

An `<li>` element always has a `<p>` element inside it, unless it's a child of `<ul class=brief>`.

If a "block" element contains a single "block" element, do not put it on a newline.

Do not indent for anything except a new "block" element. For instance
```html
 <li><p>For each <var>token</var> in <var>tokens</var>, in given order, that is not in
 <a>tokens</a>, append <var>token</var> to <a>tokens</a>.
```
is not indented, but
```html
<ol>
 <li>
  <p>For each <var>token</var> in <var>tokens</var>, run these substeps:

  <ol>
   <li><p>If <var>token</var> is the empty string, <a>throw</a> a {{SyntaxError}} exception.
```
is.

End tags may be included (if done consistently) and attributes may be quoted (using double quotes), though the prevalent theme is to omit end tags and not quote attributes (unless they contain a space).

Place one newline between paragraphs (including list elements). Place three newlines before `<h2>`, and two newlines before other headings. This does not apply when a nested heading follows the parent heading.
```html
<ul>
 <li><p>Do not place a newline above.

 <li><p>Place a newline above.
</ul>

<p>Place a newline above.


<h3>Place two newlines above.</h3>

<h4>Placing one newline is OK here.</h4>


<h4>Place two newlines above.</h4>
```
Use camel-case for variable names and "spaced" names for definitions, algorithms, etc.
```html
<p>A <a for=/>request</a> has an associated
<dfn export for=request id=concept-request-redirect-mode>redirect mode</dfn>,...
```
```html
<p>Let <var>redirectMode</var> be <var>request</var>'s <a for=request>redirect mode</a>.
```
<!-- wp:heading {"level":1} -->
<h1 class="wp-block-heading">ค่า rel ที่มีอยู่</h1>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#mw-head">กระโดดไปที่การนําทาง</a><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#searchInput">กระโดดไปค้นหา</a></p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p><br>หน้านี้ประกอบด้วยตารางค่าความสัมพันธ์ HTML ที่ทราบจากข้อกําหนด รูปแบบ ข้อเสนอ การระดมความคิด และไม่สําคัญ&nbsp;<a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/POSH">หรูหรา</a>&nbsp;การใช้งานในป่า นอกจากนี้ ค่าที่ตกหล่นและปฏิเสธจะแสดงไว้ท้ายรายการเพื่อความครอบคลุม</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>การใช้งาน: ดู&nbsp;<a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-faq#How_is_rel_used">'rel' ใช้อย่างไร</a>● ว่าด้วย&nbsp;รายได้, ดู:&nbsp;<a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-faq#Should_rev_even_be_used">ควรใช้ 'rev' ด้วยซ้ํา</a>●</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>หน้านี้ยังเป็นรีจิสทรี rel อย่างเป็นทางการ (<a href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/links.html#other-link-types">WHATWG HTML</a>&nbsp;(<a href="http://www.whatwg.org/specs/web-apps/current-work/multipage/links.html#other-link-types">ลิงค์ต้นฉบับ</a>),&nbsp;<a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html5/links.html#other-link-types">W3C HTML5</a>) เพิ่มค่า rel ใหม่และที่เสนอในส่วนต่อไปนี้:</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#HTML5_link_type_extensions">ส่วนขยายประเภทลิงก์ HTML5</a></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list -->

<!-- wp:heading -->
<h2 class="wp-block-heading">รูปแบบ</h2>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>ค่า rel เหล่านี้เป็นรูปแบบที่กําหนดจากข้อกําหนดเฉพาะ (HTML 4, ไมโครฟอร์แมต) จึงเป็นเช่นนี้&nbsp;<strong>แนะนําสําหรับการใช้งานทั่วไป</strong>● เรียงตามตัวอักษรตามมูลค่า</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>หมายเหตุ:</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>อย่า</strong> เพิ่มค่า rel ที่เสนอสําหรับ HTML5 ที่นี่ เพิ่มลงใน <a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#HTML5_link_type_extensions">ส่วนขยายประเภทลิงก์ HTML5</a> ตาราง</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>อย่า</strong> เพิ่มค่า rel ที่คุณพบใน wild ลงในตารางของรูปแบบ rel นี้ แทนที่จะเพิ่มลงในตารางใน <a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#POSH_usage">ส่วนหรู</a>●</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>อย่า</strong> เพิ่มค่า rel ที่ไม่ใช่ HTML ที่คุณพบในตารางของรูปแบบ rel นี้ แทนที่จะเพิ่มลงในตารางใน <a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#non_HTML_rel_values">ส่วนค่า rel ที่ไม่ใช่ HTML</a>●</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>อย่า</strong> เพิ่มค่า rel จากข้อเสนอหรือร่างที่ล้าสมัย/เหนือกว่า แทนที่จะเพิ่มลงในตารางในส่วน "ดรอป"</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>แหล่งที่มา:</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li>คําแนะนํา W3C:<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html401/types.html#h-6.12">HTML 4.01 ส่วนที่ 6.12 ประเภทลิงก์</a> (ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4)</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/grddl/">การรวบรวมคําอธิบายทรัพยากรจากภาษาถิ่นของภาษา</a> (GRDDL)</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list --></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/microformats">ไมโครฟอร์แมต</a> ข้อมูลจําเพาะ<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/xfn">เอ็กซ์เอฟเอ็น</a></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-license">rel-ใบอนุญาต</a></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-nofollow">rel-nofollow</a></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list --></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list -->

"ถัดไป" ล่วงหน้า เพื่อลดเวลาในการโหลดที่รับรู้</td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html4/types.html#h-6.12">ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-nofollow">ไม่ติดตาม</a></td><td>ไม่อนุญาต</td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>แสดงว่าปลายทางของไฮเปอร์ลิงก์นั้น&nbsp;<em>ไม่ควร</em>&nbsp;ได้รับน้ําหนักเพิ่มเติมใด ๆ หรือการจัดอันดับโดยตัวแทนผู้ใช้ซึ่งดําเนินการวิเคราะห์การเชื่อมโยงบนหน้าเว็บ (เช่นเครื่องมือค้นหา)</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-nofollow">rel-nofollow</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-parent&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">ผู้ปกครอง</a></td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>บุคคลที่อ้างอิงคือผู้ปกครองของบุคคลที่แสดงโดยเอกสารปัจจุบัน นอกจากนี้ยังอาจบ่งชี้ว่าเอกสารเป้าหมายเป็นพาเรนต์แบบลําดับชั้นหรือคอนเทนเนอร์ของเอกสารปัจจุบัน</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/XFN">เอ็กซ์เอฟเอ็น</a>,&nbsp;<a href="https://www.w3.org/TR/relations.html">ข้อมูลจําเพาะ HTML 4.0</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-prev">ก่อนหน้า</a></td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>หมายถึงเอกสารก่อนหน้าในชุดเอกสารที่เรียงลําดับ ตัวแทนผู้ใช้บางรายยังรองรับคําพ้องความหมาย "ก่อนหน้า"</td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html4/types.html#h-6.12">ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-previous&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">ก่อนหน้า</a></td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>คําพ้องความหมาย&nbsp;<code>ก่อนหน้า</code></td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html4/types.html#h-6.12">ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-section&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">ส่วน</a></td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>หมายถึง เอกสารที่ทําหน้าที่เป็นส่วนหนึ่งในชุดเอกสาร</td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html4/types.html#h-6.12">ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-sibling&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">พี่น้อง</a></td><td>ไม่อนุญาต</td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>บุคคลที่อ้างอิงเป็นพี่น้องของบุคคลที่แสดงโดยเอกสารปัจจุบัน</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/XFN">เอ็กซ์เอฟเอ็น</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-spouse&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">คู่สมรส</a></td><td>ไม่อนุญาต</td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>บุคคลที่อ้างอิงคือคู่สมรสของบุคคลที่แสดงโดยเอกสารปัจจุบัน</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/XFN">เอ็กซ์เอฟเอ็น</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-start&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">เริ่มต้น</a></td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>หมายถึงเอกสารฉบับแรกในชุดเอกสาร ลิงก์ประเภทนี้จะบอกเครื่องมือค้นหาว่าเอกสารใดที่ผู้เขียนพิจารณาว่าเป็นจุดเริ่มต้นของคอลเลกชัน</td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html4/types.html#h-6.12">ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-stylesheet&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">สไตล์ชีต</a></td><td>ทรัพยากรภายนอก</td><td>ไม่อนุญาต</td><td>สไตล์ชีตสําหรับเอกสารปัจจุบัน<br>ใช้กับองค์ประกอบ &lt;link href&gt; ที่มองไม่เห็นซึ่งไม่เหมาะสําหรับความสัมพันธ์ของเนื้อหา ความสัมพันธ์ของเนื้อหาควรให้ผู้ใช้มองเห็นได้ ดังนั้นจึงควรใช้ &lt;a href&gt; เป็นอย่างยิ่ง น่าเสียดายที่การใช้สไตล์ชีตในเนื้อหาที่ผู้ใช้มองเห็นได้ เช่น &lt;a href&gt; ดูเหมือนจะเป็นไปตามทฤษฎีอย่างเคร่งครัด</td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html4/types.html#h-6.12">ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-subsection&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">ส่วนย่อย</a></td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>หมายถึงเอกสารที่ทําหน้าที่เป็นส่วนย่อยในการรวบรวมเอกสาร</td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html4/types.html#h-6.12">ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-sweetheart">หวานใจ</a></td><td>ไม่อนุญาต</td><td>ความสัมพันธ์ภายนอก</td><td>บุคคลนี้ถือว่าบุคคลที่ถูกอ้างอิงเป็นคู่รักของพวกเขา</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/XFN">เอ็กซ์เอฟเอ็น</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-tag">แท็ก</a></td><td>ไม่อนุญาต</td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>... บ่งชี้ว่า [เอกสารอ้างอิง] เป็น "แท็ก" ที่ผู้เขียนกําหนด (หรือคําหลัก/หัวเรื่อง) สําหรับหน้าปัจจุบัน</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-tag">rel-แท็ก</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-toc&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">ต๊อก</a></td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>คําพ้องความหมาย&nbsp;<code>เนื้อหา</code>&nbsp;(จาก "สารบัญ")</td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html4/types.html#h-6.12">ประเภทลิงก์ HTML4</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-transformation&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">การแปลง</a></td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>อนุญาต</td><td>เชื่อมโยงเอกสารต้นฉบับกับการแปลง ซึ่งโดยปกติจะแสดงเป็น XSLT ที่เกี่ยวข้องกับไวยากรณ์เอกสารต้นฉบับกับไวยากรณ์กราฟ RDF ใช้ใน&nbsp;<a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/grddl">GRDDL</a></td><td><a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/grddl/#transformation">GRDDL</a></td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
<!-- /wp:table -->

<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">หมายเหตุ</h3>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><code>rel="สํารอง"</code> สามารถใช้ความหมายเพิ่มเติมจากแอตทริบิวต์เพิ่มเติมได้ เช่น<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><code>rel="ทางเลือก" lang="fr"</code> (หน้านี้เวอร์ชั่นภาษาฝรั่งเศส)</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><code>rel="สื่อสํารอง"="สิ่งพิมพ์"</code> (เวอร์ชันที่พิมพ์ได้ของหน้านี้)</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><code>rel="สื่อสํารอง"="มือถือ"</code> (เวอร์ชันของหน้าที่ตั้งใจหรือดีกว่าสําหรับอุปกรณ์พกพา/พกพา เช่น PDA โทรศัพท์มือถือ ฯลฯ)</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list --></li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li>คําพ้องความหมายเช่น "ก่อนหน้า", "toc" ไม่ได้รับการสนับสนุนอย่างกว้างขวางเท่ากับคําหลัก</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list -->

<!-- wp:heading -->
<h2 class="wp-block-heading">ข้อเสนอ</h2>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>ค่า rel บางส่วนได้รับการพัฒนาเป็นร่างอันเป็นผลมาจากการผ่านไมโครฟอร์แมตส่วนใหญ่&nbsp;<a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/process">กระบวนการ</a>, และด้วยเหตุนี้จึงระบุไว้ที่นี่เพื่อการพิจารณาอย่างจริงจังของคุณ คุณ&nbsp;<strong>อาจจะใช้ค่าเหล่านี้</strong>, และถ้าคุณพบปัญหาใด ๆ กับพวกเขาโปรดชี้ให้เห็นในหน้า "ปัญหา" ที่เกี่ยวข้องสําหรับค่า rel</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:table -->
<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><tbody><tr><th>ค่าความสัมพันธ์</th><th>สรุป</th><th>เสนอใน</th><th>สเปคภายนอก (ถ้ามี)</th></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-pronunciation">การออกเสียง</a></td><td>... บ่งชี้ว่าปลายทางขององค์ประกอบ 'เชื่อมโยง' เป็นเอกสารที่ให้พจนานุกรมการออกเสียงสําหรับวัตถุประสงค์ในการสังเคราะห์เสียงพูด</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-pronunciation">การออกเสียง rel</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-directory">ไดเรกทอรี</a></td><td>... บ่งชี้ว่าปลายทางของไฮเปอร์ลิงก์เป็นรายการไดเรกทอรีที่มีรายการสําหรับหน้าปัจจุบัน</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-directory">ไดเร็กทอรี rel</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-enclosure">สิ่งที่แนบมา</a></td><td>... บ่งชี้ว่าปลายทางของไฮเปอร์ลิงก์นั้นมีวัตถุประสงค์เพื่อดาวน์โหลดและแคช</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-enclosure">rel-สิ่งที่แนบมา</a></td><td><a href="http://www.apps.ietf.org/rfc/rfc4287.html">อาร์เอฟซี4287</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-home">บ้าน</a></td><td>... บ่งชี้ว่า [เอกสารอ้างอิง] เป็นหน้าแรกของไซต์ที่หน้าปัจจุบันปรากฏขึ้น</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-home">rel-บ้าน</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-payment">การชําระเงิน</a></td><td>... บ่งชี้ว่าปลายทางของไฮเปอร์ลิงก์มีวิธีการแสดงหรือให้การสนับสนุน (เช่น การเงิน) สําหรับหน้าปัจจุบัน</td><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-payment">การชําระเงินซ้ํา</a></td></tr><tr><td>วีซีเอส-*</td><td>... บ่งชี้ว่าปลายทางของไฮเปอร์ลิงก์คือตําแหน่งของที่เก็บ Version Control System ที่เกี่ยวข้องกับหน้านั้น</td><td></td><td><a href="https://joeyh.name/rfc/rel-vcs/">rel=vcs-*</a></td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
<!-- /wp:table -->

<!-- wp:heading -->
<h2 class="wp-block-heading">ส่วนขยายประเภทลิงก์ HTML5</h2>
<!-- /wp:heading -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>ค่าต่อไปนี้ได้รับการลงทะเบียนเป็นส่วนขยายประเภทลิงก์ตาม&nbsp;<a href="http://www.whatwg.org/specs/web-apps/current-work/multipage/links.html#other-link-types">ข้อกําหนดในข้อกําหนด HTML WHATWG</a>&nbsp;และ&nbsp;<a href="http://www.w3.org/TR/html5/links.html#other-link-types">ข้อกําหนดในข้อกําหนด W3C HTML5</a>●</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p><br>ก่อนที่คุณจะลงทะเบียนค่าใหม่:</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>กรุณาตรวจสอบ <a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#formats">ตารางรูปแบบ</a> และอย่าลงทะเบียนใหม่</strong> ค่า REL ที่มีอยู่แล้ว โปรดทราบว่า W3C HTML WG ได้สร้าง <a href="http://lists.w3.org/Archives/Public/public-html/2011Feb/att-0481/issue-118-decision.html">การตัดสินใจ</a> ที่จะลดลง <code>ดัชนี</code>, <code>ขึ้น</code>, <code>ก่อน</code> และ <code>สุดท้าย</code> จากสเปค HTML5 นั้นเอง</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li><strong>กรุณาตรวจสอบ <a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#dropped">โต๊ะหล่น</a> และอย่าลงทะเบียน</strong> ค่าที่มีอยู่แล้ว หากคุณเชื่อว่าค่าที่เกี่ยวข้องถูกละทิ้งจากข้อกําหนดอื่นโดยไม่มีอคติ โปรดระบุลิงก์/อ้างอิงถึงข้อความ/การตัดสินใจที่ชัดเจนโดยระบุเช่นนั้น เช่น ค่าดังกล่าวเป็นเพียงการเลื่อนออกไป หรืออาจคาดว่าจะถูกแยกออกเป็นข้อกําหนดเฉพาะของตนเองจากกลุ่ม การพัฒนาข้อกําหนดนั้น</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>โปรดทราบว่ารายการใน&nbsp;<a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/existing-rel-values#formats">ตารางรูปแบบ</a>&nbsp;และรายการที่อยู่ใน HTML5 เป็นคําหลักในตัวก็ถือเป็นส่วนขยายที่มีสถานะ "ให้สัตยาบัน" เช่นกัน</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>โปรดตรวจสอบให้แน่ใจว่าการลงทะเบียนที่เพิ่มที่นี่มีข้อมูลที่จําเป็นทั้งหมดกรอกแล้ว&nbsp;<em>รวมทั้ง</em>:</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:list -->
<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
<li>"ผลกระทบต่อการเชื่อมโยง"</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li>"ผลกระทบต่อพื้นที่และพื้นที่" และ</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item -->

<!-- wp:list-item -->
<li>ลิงก์ไปยังข้อมูลจําเพาะที่บันทึกคําหลัก <em>เป็น HTML <code>เรล</code> คําหลัก</em>● (ข้อกําหนดที่กําหนดเพียงรูปแบบไฟล์ของเป้าหมายลิงก์ แต่ไม่ได้กําหนด <code>เรล</code> keyword สําหรับใช้ในภาษา HTML ไม่ใช่สเปคแบบที่ต้องการในที่นี้)</li>
<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
<!-- /wp:list -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>รายการที่ไม่มีข้อมูลที่จําเป็นข้างต้นมีแนวโน้มที่จะถูกลบออก</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:paragraph -->
<p>การเปลี่ยนแปลงรีจิสทรีนี้จะไม่แสดงในตัวตรวจสอบแบบเรียลไทม์ แต่โดยทั่วไปผู้ตรวจสอบจะได้รับการอัปเดตโดยอัตโนมัติพร้อมการเปลี่ยนแปลงภายในหนึ่งสัปดาห์หรือประมาณนั้น</p>
<!-- /wp:paragraph -->

<!-- wp:table -->
<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><tbody><tr><th>คําหลัก</th><th>ผลต่อ&nbsp;<code>ลิงค์</code></th><th>ผลต่อ&nbsp;<code>ก</code>,&nbsp;<code>พื้นที่</code></th><th>คําอธิบายสั้น ๆ</th><th>เชื่อมโยงไปยังข้อกําหนด</th><th>คําพ้องความหมาย</th><th>สถานะ</th></tr><tr><td><a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/index.php?title=rel-about&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1">เกี่ยวกับ</a></td><td>ไฮเปอร์ลิงค์</td><td>ไฮเปอร์ลิงค์</td><td>ทรัพยากรที่เชื่อมโยงกับมีข้อมูลเกี่ยวกับหน้าปัจจุบัน</td><td><a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6903.html#section-2">RFC6903</a></td><td>มันอาจจะรวมกับ&nbsp;<a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-me">เรล-มี</a>&nbsp;หรือ&nbsp;<a href="https://microformats.org/wiki/rel-author">ผู้เขียนซ้ํา</a>&nbsp;ในลิงก์ไปยังส่วน "เกี่ยวกับ" ที่เน้นไปที่ผู้เขียน</td><td>เสนอ</td></tr><tr><td><a
